\#Data Pre-processing:

In this step, we want to:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Read the data
\end{enumerate}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{ev\_data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{read.csv}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Data/EV\_Charging/EV\_Charging\_Data{-}\_Municipal\_Lots\_and\_Garages\_20251106.csv"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{stringsAsFactors =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{check.names =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\setcounter{enumi}{1}
\tightlist
\item
  Choose ID tag as an unique identifier:
\end{enumerate}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Choose ID\_tag as unique identifier Check if ID\_tag is}
\CommentTok{\# unique}
\FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{duplicated}\NormalTok{(ev\_data}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{ID Tag}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{))}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## [1] 176776
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Keep only the first row per ID\_tag}
\NormalTok{ev\_data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ev\_data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{distinct}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{ID Tag}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{.keep\_all =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\setcounter{enumi}{2}
\tightlist
\item
  Remove Columns and rows that are not relevent to our experiment:
\end{enumerate}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Remove columns: Driver\_id, Invalidity\_reason, country}
\NormalTok{ev\_data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ev\_data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Driver ID}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{, }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Invalidity Reason}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{, }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{Country)}

\CommentTok{\# Remove rows where session\_status is “Disconnected”}
\NormalTok{ev\_data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ev\_data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Session Status}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}} \SpecialCharTok{!=} \StringTok{"DISCONNECTED"}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Keep Station\_name and drop Charger\_id}
\NormalTok{ev\_data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ev\_data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Charge Box ID}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Check null values and drop rows with any NA}
\FunctionTok{colSums}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(ev\_data))}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
##                     Date             Station Name            Location Name 
##                        0                        0                        0 
##             Connector ID                   ID Tag           Connected Time 
##                        0                        0                        0 
##        Disconnected Time    Charge Duration (min) Connected Duration (min) 
##                        0                        0                        0 
##    Energy Provided (kWh)           Session Status 
##                        0                        0
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{ev\_data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ev\_data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
\NormalTok{    tidyr}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{drop\_na}\NormalTok{()}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

4.Convert type from chr to numeric

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# change Charge Duration (min) to numeric}
\NormalTok{ev\_data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ev\_data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Charge Duration (min)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}} \OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{gsub}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{","}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Charge Duration (min)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{)))}
\CommentTok{\# change Connected Duration (min) to numeric}
\NormalTok{ev\_data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ev\_data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Connected Duration (min)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}} \OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{gsub}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{","}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Connected Duration (min)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{)))}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\setcounter{enumi}{4}
\tightlist
\item
  Add columns based on Dates
\end{enumerate}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Sort the data based on date}
\NormalTok{ev\_data}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Date }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.Date}\NormalTok{(ev\_data}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Date, }\AttributeTok{format =} \StringTok{"\%m/\%d/\%Y"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{ev\_data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ev\_data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(Date)}

\CommentTok{\# Add weekday column}
\NormalTok{ev\_data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ev\_data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{weekday =} \FunctionTok{weekdays}\NormalTok{(Date))}

\CommentTok{\# Add is\_weekend}
\NormalTok{weekends }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Saturday"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Sunday"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{ev\_data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ev\_data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{is\_weekend =}\NormalTok{ weekday }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ weekends}
\NormalTok{  )}

\CommentTok{\# Add holidays column for year 2024}
\CommentTok{\# Need to work on this!!}
\NormalTok{holidays\_2024 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.Date}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
  \StringTok{"2024{-}01{-}01"}\NormalTok{, }\CommentTok{\# New Year\textquotesingle{}s Day}
  \StringTok{"2024{-}01{-}15"}\NormalTok{, }\CommentTok{\# MLK Day}
  \StringTok{"2024{-}02{-}19"}\NormalTok{, }\CommentTok{\# Presidents\textquotesingle{} Day}
  \StringTok{"2024{-}05{-}27"}\NormalTok{, }\CommentTok{\# Memorial Day}
  \StringTok{"2024{-}06{-}19"}\NormalTok{, }\CommentTok{\# Juneteenth}
  \StringTok{"2024{-}07{-}04"}\NormalTok{, }\CommentTok{\# Independence Day}
  \StringTok{"2024{-}09{-}02"}\NormalTok{, }\CommentTok{\# Labor Day}
  \StringTok{"2024{-}10{-}14"}\NormalTok{, }\CommentTok{\# Columbus/Indigenous Peoples\textquotesingle{} Day}
  \StringTok{"2024{-}11{-}11"}\NormalTok{, }\CommentTok{\# Veterans Day}
  \StringTok{"2024{-}11{-}28"}\NormalTok{, }\CommentTok{\# Thanksgiving}
  \StringTok{"2024{-}12{-}25"}  \CommentTok{\# Christmas}
\NormalTok{))}

\NormalTok{ev\_data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ev\_data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{is\_holiday\_2024 =}\NormalTok{ Date }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ holidays\_2024}
\NormalTok{  )}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\setcounter{enumi}{5}
\tightlist
\item
  Clean the location names:
\end{enumerate}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Standardize location names}
\NormalTok{ev\_data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ev\_data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}} \OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(}
      \CommentTok{\# Jerome Gun Hill Road variations}
      \FunctionTok{grepl}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"JGU|Jerome.*Gun Hill"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{ignore.case =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} 
        \StringTok{"Jerome Gun Hill Road Municipal Parking Garage"}\NormalTok{,}
      
      \CommentTok{\# Bay Ridge variations}
      \FunctionTok{grepl}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"BRI|Bay Ridge"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{ignore.case =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} 
        \StringTok{"Bay Ridge Municipal Parking Garage"}\NormalTok{,}
      
      \CommentTok{\# Delancey and Essex variations}
      \FunctionTok{grepl}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"DES|Delancey.*Essex"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{ignore.case =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} 
        \StringTok{"Delancey and Essex Municipal Parking Garage"}\NormalTok{,}
      
      \CommentTok{\# Queens Borough Hall variations}
      \FunctionTok{grepl}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"QBO|Queensboro Hall|Queens Borough Hall"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{ignore.case =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} 
        \StringTok{"Queens Borough Hall Municipal Parking Garage"}\NormalTok{,}
      
      \CommentTok{\# Jerome 190th Street variations}
      \FunctionTok{grepl}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"JON|Jerome.*190"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{ignore.case =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} 
        \StringTok{"Jerome 190th Street Municipal Parking Garage"}\NormalTok{,}
      
      \CommentTok{\# St. George Courthouse variations}
      \FunctionTok{grepl}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"SGE|St}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}\textbackslash{}}\StringTok{.? George"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{ignore.case =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} 
        \StringTok{"St. George Courthouse Garage"}\NormalTok{,}
      
      \CommentTok{\# Court Square variations}
      \FunctionTok{grepl}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"CSQ|Court Square"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{ignore.case =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} 
        \StringTok{"Court Square Municipal Parking Garage"}\NormalTok{,}
      
      \CommentTok{\# Queens Family Court variations}
      \FunctionTok{grepl}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"QFA|Queens Family Court"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{ignore.case =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} 
        \StringTok{"Queens Family Court Municipal Garage"}\NormalTok{,}
      
      \CommentTok{\# Handle N/A}
      \StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}} \SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"N/A"} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{NA\_character\_}\NormalTok{,}
      
      \CommentTok{\# Keep everything else as is}
      \ConstantTok{TRUE} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{  )}

\CommentTok{\# Check the results}
\FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{(ev\_data}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\section{Descriptive Analysis}\label{descriptive-analysis}

\subsubsection{Frequency Tables}\label{frequency-tables}

1.⁠ ⁠Frequency table → Session Status distribution; Day of the week
distribution; Weekday vs.~Weekend distribution; Location distribution;
Charging Start Hours distribution; Charging End Hours distribution

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 1. Session Status distribution}
\NormalTok{session\_status\_freq }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ev\_data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{count}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Session Status}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{sort =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{percentage =}\NormalTok{ n }\SpecialCharTok{/} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(n) }\SpecialCharTok{*} \DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{percentage =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(percentage, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  )}

\CommentTok{\# Session Status bar chart}
\FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(session\_status\_freq, }\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =} \FunctionTok{reorder}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Session Status}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{, }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{n), }\AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ n)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{geom\_bar}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{stat =} \StringTok{"identity"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{fill =} \StringTok{"steelblue"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{geom\_text}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{label =} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(n, }\StringTok{" ("}\NormalTok{, percentage, }\StringTok{"\%)"}\NormalTok{)), }\AttributeTok{vjust =} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\FloatTok{0.5}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \CommentTok{\# Label of count and percentage}
  \FunctionTok{scale\_y\_continuous}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{expand =} \FunctionTok{expansion}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{mult =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.15}\NormalTok{))) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \CommentTok{\# Additional space to account for labels}
  \FunctionTok{labs}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"Session Status Distribution"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{x =} \StringTok{"Status"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{y =} \StringTok{"Count"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\DocumentationTok{\#\#\# Center and Dispersion Statistics}

\CommentTok{\# Function to calculate mode (R doesn\textquotesingle{}t have a built{-}in mode function)}
\NormalTok{get\_mode }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(x) \{}
\NormalTok{  x }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{na.omit}\NormalTok{(x)}
\NormalTok{  ux }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{(x)}
\NormalTok{  ux[}\FunctionTok{which.max}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{tabulate}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{match}\NormalTok{(x, ux)))]}
\NormalTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Function to calculate all statistics for a variable}
\NormalTok{calc\_stats }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(x, var\_name) \{}
\NormalTok{  x }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{na.omit}\NormalTok{(x)}
  
  \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{Variable =}\NormalTok{ var\_name,}
    \AttributeTok{Mean =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(x), }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{Median =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{median}\NormalTok{(x), }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{Mode =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{get\_mode}\NormalTok{(x), }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{Trimmed\_Mean\_10pct =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(x, }\AttributeTok{trim =} \FloatTok{0.10}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{IQR =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{IQR}\NormalTok{(x), }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{Variance =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{var}\NormalTok{(x), }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{Std\_Dev =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sd}\NormalTok{(x), }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{CV =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{((}\FunctionTok{sd}\NormalTok{(x) }\SpecialCharTok{/} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(x)) }\SpecialCharTok{*} \DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),  }\CommentTok{\# Coefficient of Variation as percentage}
    \AttributeTok{Skewness =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{skewness}\NormalTok{(x), }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Calculate statistics for Energy Provided and Charge Duration}
\NormalTok{energy\_stats }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{calc\_stats}\NormalTok{(ev\_data}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Energy Provided (kWh)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Energy Provided (kWh)"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{duration\_stats }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{calc\_stats}\NormalTok{(ev\_data}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Charge Duration (min)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Charge Duration (min)"}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Combine into one table}
\NormalTok{summary\_stats }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{rbind}\NormalTok{(energy\_stats, duration\_stats)}

\CommentTok{\# Display the table}
\FunctionTok{kable}\NormalTok{(summary\_stats, }
      \AttributeTok{caption =} \StringTok{"Summary Statistics: Measures of Center and Dispersion"}\NormalTok{,}
      \AttributeTok{col.names =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Variable"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Mean"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Median"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Mode"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Trimmed Mean (10\%)"}\NormalTok{, }
                    \StringTok{"IQR"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Variance"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Std Dev"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"CV (\%)"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Skewness"}\NormalTok{),}
      \AttributeTok{align =} \StringTok{"lrrrrrrrrr"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{kable\_styling}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{bootstrap\_options =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"striped"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"hover"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"condensed"}\NormalTok{),}
                \AttributeTok{full\_width =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{,}
                \AttributeTok{position =} \StringTok{"center"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{longtable}[t]{lrrrrrrrrr}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Total Energy Consumed by Day of Week}
\NormalTok{energy\_by\_day }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ev\_data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(weekday) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{total\_energy =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Energy Provided (kWh)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{num\_sessions =} \FunctionTok{n}\NormalTok{(), }\AttributeTok{avg\_energy =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Energy Provided (kWh)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{total\_energy\_rounded =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(total\_energy, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{avg\_energy\_rounded =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(avg\_energy,}
        \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{))}

\CommentTok{\# Bar plot of Total Energy by Weekday}
\FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(energy\_by\_day, }\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ weekday, }\AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ total\_energy, }\AttributeTok{fill =}\NormalTok{ weekday)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{geom\_bar}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{stat =} \StringTok{"identity"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{geom\_text}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{label =} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{format}\NormalTok{(total\_energy\_rounded,}
    \AttributeTok{big.mark =} \StringTok{","}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{" kWh"}\NormalTok{)), }\AttributeTok{vjust =} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\FloatTok{0.5}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{size =} \FloatTok{3.5}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{scale\_y\_continuous}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{expand =} \FunctionTok{expansion}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{mult =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{,}
    \FloatTok{0.15}\NormalTok{))) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{labs}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"Total Energy Consumed by Day of Week"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{x =} \StringTok{"Day of Week"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{y =} \StringTok{"Total Energy (kWh)"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{theme\_minimal}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{legend.position =} \StringTok{"none"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{axis.text.x =} \FunctionTok{element\_text}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{angle =} \DecValTok{45}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{hjust =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{scale\_fill\_brewer}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{palette =} \StringTok{"Set2"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}

\FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(energy\_by\_location, }\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =} \FunctionTok{reorder}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{, total\_energy),}
    \AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ total\_energy, }\AttributeTok{fill =} \StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{geom\_bar}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{stat =} \StringTok{"identity"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{geom\_text}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{label =} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{format}\NormalTok{(total\_energy\_rounded,}
        \AttributeTok{big.mark =} \StringTok{","}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{" kWh"}\NormalTok{)), }\AttributeTok{hjust =} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\FloatTok{0.1}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{size =} \FloatTok{3.5}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{coord\_flip}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{scale\_y\_continuous}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{expand =} \FunctionTok{expansion}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{mult =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{,}
    \FloatTok{0.2}\NormalTok{))) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{labs}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"Total Energy Consumed by Location"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{x =} \StringTok{"Location"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{y =} \StringTok{"Total Energy (kWh)"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{theme\_minimal}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{legend.position =} \StringTok{"none"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{scale\_fill\_brewer}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{palette =} \StringTok{"Set3"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\DocumentationTok{\#\#\# Heatmap of Temporal Usage (Weekday × Hour of Day)}

\CommentTok{\# Prepare data: extract hour and summarize}
\NormalTok{temporal\_data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ev\_data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{time\_object =} \FunctionTok{hms}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Connected Time}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{hour =} \FunctionTok{hour}\NormalTok{(time\_object)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(hour)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(weekday, hour) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{num\_sessions =} \FunctionTok{n}\NormalTok{(), }\AttributeTok{avg\_energy =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Energy Provided (kWh)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{total\_energy =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Energy Provided (kWh)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{.groups =} \StringTok{"drop"}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Ensure proper weekday order}
\NormalTok{temporal\_data}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{weekday }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(temporal\_data}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{weekday, }\AttributeTok{levels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Monday"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"Tuesday"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Wednesday"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Thursday"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Friday"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Saturday"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"Sunday"}\NormalTok{))}

\CommentTok{\# 1. Heatmap: Number of Sessions by Weekday × Hour}
\FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(temporal\_data, }\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ hour, }\AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ weekday, }\AttributeTok{fill =}\NormalTok{ num\_sessions)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{geom\_tile}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{color =} \StringTok{"white"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{geom\_text}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{label =}\NormalTok{ num\_sessions),}
    \AttributeTok{size =} \FloatTok{2.5}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{color =} \StringTok{"black"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{scale\_fill\_gradient}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{low =} \StringTok{"lightyellow"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{high =} \StringTok{"darkred"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{scale\_x\_continuous}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{breaks =} \DecValTok{0}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{23}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{labs}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"Number of Charging Sessions by Day and Hour"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{subtitle =} \StringTok{"Identifying peak charging times"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{x =} \StringTok{"Hour of Day (24h)"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{y =} \StringTok{"Day of Week"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{fill =} \StringTok{"Sessions"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{theme\_minimal}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{panel.grid =} \FunctionTok{element\_blank}\NormalTok{())}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\#Inferencial Statistics:

\subsubsection{Weekend vs weekday
differences}\label{weekend-vs-weekday-differences}

\begin{itemize}
\item
  Two-sample t-tests (or Wilcoxon based on normality) on Charge Duration
  and Energy Provided:
\item
  H0: Mean of Weekend = Mean of Weekdays.
\item
  95\% CI for the difference in means.
\item
  Have to check for normality (or lack of it) using the histograms +
  Q--Q plots from desc stats.
\end{itemize}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\DocumentationTok{\#\#\# Weekend vs Weekday Differences}

\CommentTok{\# Separate data into weekend and weekday groups}
\NormalTok{weekday\_data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ev\_data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(is\_weekend }\SpecialCharTok{==} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{weekend\_data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ev\_data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(is\_weekend }\SpecialCharTok{==} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Display sample sizes}
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Sample Sizes:}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

## Sample Sizes:
\end{verbatim}

\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# 1. Normality Check: Histograms}
\CommentTok{\#{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}

\CommentTok{\# Histogram: Energy Provided by Weekend vs Weekday}
\FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(ev\_data, }\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =} \StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Energy Provided (kWh)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{fill =}\NormalTok{ is\_weekend)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{geom\_histogram}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{bins =} \DecValTok{30}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{alpha =} \FloatTok{0.7}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{position =} \StringTok{"identity"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{facet\_wrap}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(is\_weekend, }\StringTok{"Weekend"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Weekday"}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{scales =} \StringTok{"free\_y"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{labs}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"Distribution of Energy Provided: Weekday vs Weekend"}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{x =} \StringTok{"Energy Provided (kWh)"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{y =} \StringTok{"Count"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{theme\_minimal}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{legend.position =} \StringTok{"none"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{scale\_fill\_manual}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{values =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"steelblue"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"coral"}\NormalTok{))}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

    \FunctionTok{geom\_histogram}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{bins =} \DecValTok{30}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{alpha =} \FloatTok{0.7}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{position =} \StringTok{"identity"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{facet\_wrap}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(is\_weekend, }\StringTok{"Weekend"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Weekday"}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{scales =} \StringTok{"free\_y"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{labs}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"Distribution of Charge Duration: Weekday vs Weekend"}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{x =} \StringTok{"Charge Duration (min)"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{y =} \StringTok{"Count"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{theme\_minimal}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{legend.position =} \StringTok{"none"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{scale\_fill\_manual}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{values =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"steelblue"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"coral"}\NormalTok{))}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# 3. Formal Normality Test (Shapiro{-}Wilk on sample)}
\CommentTok{\#{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# Note: Shapiro{-}Wilk limited to 5000 observations, so we}
\CommentTok{\# sample}

\FunctionTok{set.seed}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{123}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{shapiro\_weekday\_energy }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{shapiro.test}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(weekday\_data}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Energy Provided (kWh)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{,}
    \DecValTok{5000}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{shapiro\_weekend\_energy }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{shapiro.test}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(weekend\_data}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Energy Provided (kWh)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{,}
    \FunctionTok{min}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{5000}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(weekend\_data))))}
\NormalTok{shapiro\_weekday\_duration }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{shapiro.test}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(weekday\_data}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Charge Duration (min)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{,}
    \DecValTok{5000}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{shapiro\_weekend\_duration }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{shapiro.test}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(weekend\_data}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Charge Duration (min)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{,}
    \FunctionTok{min}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{5000}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(weekend\_data))))}

\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{{-}{-}{-} Shapiro{-}Wilk Normality Tests {-}{-}{-}}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\CommentTok{\#{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# 3. Formal Normality Test (Shapiro{-}Wilk)}
\CommentTok{\#{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# Note: Shapiro{-}Wilk limited to 5000 observations, so we}
\CommentTok{\# sample}

\FunctionTok{set.seed}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{123}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{shapiro\_weekday\_energy }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{shapiro.test}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(weekday\_data}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Energy Provided (kWh)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{,}
    \DecValTok{5000}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{shapiro\_weekend\_energy }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{shapiro.test}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(weekend\_data}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Energy Provided (kWh)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{,}
    \FunctionTok{min}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{5000}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(weekend\_data))))}
\NormalTok{shapiro\_weekday\_duration }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{shapiro.test}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(weekday\_data}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Charge Duration (min)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{,}
    \DecValTok{5000}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{shapiro\_weekend\_duration }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{shapiro.test}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(weekend\_data}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Charge Duration (min)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{,}
    \FunctionTok{min}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{5000}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(weekend\_data))))}

\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{{-}{-}{-} Shapiro{-}Wilk Normality Tests (α ="}\NormalTok{, alpha, }\StringTok{") {-}{-}{-}}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (loc }\ControlFlowTok{in}\NormalTok{ top\_locations) \{}

        \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}} \SpecialCharTok{==}\NormalTok{ loc) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{pull}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Energy Provided (kWh)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{)}

    \FunctionTok{qqnorm}\NormalTok{(loc\_data, }\AttributeTok{main =} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Q{-}Q Plot:"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{substr}\NormalTok{(loc, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
        \DecValTok{25}\NormalTok{)))}
    \FunctionTok{qqline}\NormalTok{(loc\_data, }\AttributeTok{col =} \StringTok{"red"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{lwd =} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{\}}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{set.seed}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{123}\NormalTok{)}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (loc }\ControlFlowTok{in}\NormalTok{ top\_locations) \{}
\NormalTok{    loc\_data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ev\_top\_locations }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}} \SpecialCharTok{==}\NormalTok{ loc) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{pull}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Energy Provided (kWh)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{    sample\_size }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{min}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{5000}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(loc\_data))}
\NormalTok{    shapiro\_result }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{shapiro.test}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(loc\_data, sample\_size))}

    \FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{substr}\NormalTok{(loc, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{40}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{":}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
    \FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"  W ="}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(shapiro\_result}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{statistic, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{", p{-}value ="}\NormalTok{,}
        \FunctionTok{format}\NormalTok{(shapiro\_result}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p.value, }\AttributeTok{scientific =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{"→"}\NormalTok{,}
        \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(shapiro\_result}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p.value }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{ alpha, }\StringTok{"Non{-}normal"}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{"Normal"}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{\}}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Fix: Create a clean column name for ANOVA}
\NormalTok{ev\_top\_locations }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ev\_top\_locations }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{Location =} \StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Run ANOVA with clean column name}
\NormalTok{anova\_model }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{aov}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Energy Provided (kWh)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ Location, }\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ ev\_top\_locations)}
\NormalTok{anova\_result }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{summary}\NormalTok{(anova\_model)}

\FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(anova\_result)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Extract F{-}statistic and p{-}value}
\NormalTok{f\_stat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ anova\_result[[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]]}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{F value}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{p\_value\_anova }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ anova\_result[[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]]}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Pr(\textgreater{}F)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]}

\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{F{-}statistic:"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(f\_stat, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Decision:"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(p\_value\_anova }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{ alpha, }\StringTok{"Reject H0 {-} Significant difference exists between locations"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"Fail to reject H0 {-} No significant difference"}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Post{-}hoc Tukey HSD (now works with clean column name)}
\ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (p\_value\_anova }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{ alpha) \{}
    \FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{{-}{-}{-} Tukey HSD Post{-}Hoc Test {-}{-}{-}}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    tukey\_result }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{TukeyHSD}\NormalTok{(anova\_model, }\AttributeTok{conf.level =} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ alpha)}
    \FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(tukey\_result)}

    \CommentTok{\# Visualize Tukey results}
    \FunctionTok{plot}\NormalTok{(tukey\_result, }\AttributeTok{las =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{cex.axis =} \FloatTok{0.5}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{\}}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{===== KRUSKAL{-}WALLIS TEST (Non{-}parametric) =====}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{kruskal\_result }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{kruskal.test}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Energy Provided (kWh)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ ev\_top\_locations)}

\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Chi{-}squared statistic:"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(kruskal\_result}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{statistic,}
    \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}

    \StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}

\begin{Highlighting}[]

    \StringTok{"Fail to reject H0 {-} No significant difference"}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}

## Decision: Reject H0 - Significant difference exists between locations
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Post{-}hoc Dunn Test (if Kruskal{-}Wallis is significant)}
\ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (kruskal\_result}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p.value }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{ alpha) \{}
    \FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{{-}{-}{-} Dunn\textquotesingle{}s Post{-}Hoc Test (Bonferroni adjustment) {-}{-}{-}}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
    \CommentTok{\# Need to install FSA package for dunnTest}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{require}\NormalTok{(FSA))}
        \FunctionTok{install.packages}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"FSA"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{quiet =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
    \FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(FSA)}
\NormalTok{    dunn\_result }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{dunnTest}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Energy Provided (kWh)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ ev\_top\_locations, }\AttributeTok{method =} \StringTok{"bonferroni"}\NormalTok{)}
    \FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(dunn\_result)}
\NormalTok{\}}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

        \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{25}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{50}\NormalTok{, }\ConstantTok{Inf}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{labels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Low (0{-}10)"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Medium (10{-}25)"}\NormalTok{,}

\NormalTok{    ev\_top\_locations}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Energy\_Category)}

\NormalTok{summary\_tests }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{Test =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"One{-}Way ANOVA"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Kruskal{-}Wallis"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"Chi{-}Square Independence"}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{Statistic =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"F ="}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(f\_stat,}
    \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{)), }\FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"χ² ="}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(kruskal\_result}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{statistic, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{)),}
    \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"χ² ="}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(chi\_sq\_result}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{statistic, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{))), }\AttributeTok{df =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(anova\_result[[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]]}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Df[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{],}
    \StringTok{"and"}\NormalTok{, anova\_result[[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]]}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Df[}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{]), kruskal\_result}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{parameter,}
\NormalTok{    chi\_sq\_result}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{parameter), }\AttributeTok{p\_value =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{format}\NormalTok{(p\_value\_anova,}
    \AttributeTok{scientific =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{), }\FunctionTok{format}\NormalTok{(kruskal\_result}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p.value, }\AttributeTok{scientific =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \FunctionTok{format}\NormalTok{(chi\_sq\_result}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p.value, }\AttributeTok{scientific =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)), }\AttributeTok{Decision =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(p\_value\_anova }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}}
\NormalTok{    alpha, }\StringTok{"Reject H0"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Fail to reject H0"}\NormalTok{), }\FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(kruskal\_result}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p.value }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}}
\NormalTok{    alpha, }\StringTok{"Reject H0"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Fail to reject H0"}\NormalTok{), }\FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(chi\_sq\_result}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p.value }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}}
\NormalTok{    alpha, }\StringTok{"Reject H0"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Fail to reject H0"}\NormalTok{)))}

\FunctionTok{kable}\NormalTok{(summary\_tests, }\AttributeTok{caption =} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Location Differences Analysis Summary (α ="}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{    alpha, }\StringTok{")"}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{col.names =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Test"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Test Statistic"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"df"}\NormalTok{,}

Proportion inference

One-sample proportion CI and test for weekend sessions:

H0: weekend = 0.3 or some reference value.

Two-sample proportion test: compare weekend usage between two specific
locations.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\DocumentationTok{\#\#\# Proportion Inference Significance Level: α = 0.05}

\NormalTok{alpha }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{0.05}

\CommentTok{\#{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# 1. One{-}Sample Proportion: Weekend Sessions}
\CommentTok{\#{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}

\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"===== ONE{-}SAMPLE PROPORTION TEST: Weekend Sessions =====}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## ===== ONE-SAMPLE PROPORTION TEST: Weekend Sessions =====
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"H0: p\_weekend = 0.30 (30\% of sessions are on weekends)}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## H0: p_weekend = 0.30 (30% of sessions are on weekends)
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Ha: p\_weekend ≠ 0.30}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## Ha: p_weekend ≠ 0.30
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"α ="}\NormalTok{, alpha, }\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## α = 0.05
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Calculate observed proportion}
\NormalTok{n\_total }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(ev\_data)}
\NormalTok{n\_weekend }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(ev\_data}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{is\_weekend }\SpecialCharTok{==} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{p\_observed }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ n\_weekend}\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{n\_total}

\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"{-}{-}{-} Sample Statistics {-}{-}{-}}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## --- Sample Statistics ---
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Total sessions:"}\NormalTok{, n\_total, }\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## Total sessions: 23391
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Weekend sessions:"}\NormalTok{, n\_weekend, }\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## Weekend sessions: 7788
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Observed proportion (p̂):"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(p\_observed, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## Observed proportion (p̂): 0.3329
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Expected proportion (p0): 0.30}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## Expected proportion (p0): 0.30
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# One{-}sample proportion test}
\NormalTok{p0 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{0.3}  \CommentTok{\# Hypothesized proportion}
\NormalTok{prop\_test\_one }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{prop.test}\NormalTok{(n\_weekend, n\_total, }\AttributeTok{p =}\NormalTok{ p0, }\AttributeTok{conf.level =} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}
\NormalTok{    alpha, }\AttributeTok{correct =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{)  }\CommentTok{\# Without Yates continuity correction}

\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"{-}{-}{-} One{-}Sample z{-}Test for Proportion {-}{-}{-}}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## --- One-Sample z-Test for Proportion ---
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Chi{-}squared statistic:"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(prop\_test\_one}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{statistic,}
    \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## Chi-squared statistic: 120.9213
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"p{-}value:"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{format}\NormalTok{(prop\_test\_one}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p.value, }\AttributeTok{scientific =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## p-value: 3.976055e-28
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"95\% Confidence Interval for p\_weekend:"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(prop\_test\_one}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{conf.int[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{],}
    \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{"to"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(prop\_test\_one}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{conf.int[}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{], }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## 95% Confidence Interval for p_weekend: 0.3269 to 0.339
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Decision:"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(prop\_test\_one}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p.value }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{ alpha, }\StringTok{"Reject H0 {-} Weekend proportion differs from 0.30"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"Fail to reject H0 {-} No evidence weekend proportion differs from 0.30"}\NormalTok{),}
    \StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## Decision: Reject H0 - Weekend proportion differs from 0.30
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Manual z{-}test calculation for clarity}
\NormalTok{z\_stat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ (p\_observed }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ p0)}\SpecialCharTok{/}\FunctionTok{sqrt}\NormalTok{(p0 }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ p0)}\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{n\_total)}
\NormalTok{p\_value\_z }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{2} \SpecialCharTok{*} \FunctionTok{pnorm}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\FunctionTok{abs}\NormalTok{(z\_stat))  }\CommentTok{\# Two{-}tailed}

\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{{-}{-}{-} Manual z{-}Test Calculation {-}{-}{-}}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

## --- Manual z-Test Calculation ---

\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"z{-}statistic:"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(z\_stat, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

    \StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

    \AttributeTok{correct =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{)}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{conf.int}
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"95\% CI:"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(wilson\_ci[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{], }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{"to"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(wilson\_ci[}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{],}
    \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# 2. Two{-}Sample Proportion: Weekend Usage by Location}
\CommentTok{\#{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}

\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{===== TWO{-}SAMPLE PROPORTION TEST: Weekend Usage by Location =====}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
##

\CommentTok{\# Calculate proportions}

\NormalTok{p1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ x1}\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{n1}
\NormalTok{p2 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ x2}\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{n2}

Variance/spread comparisons

F-test for variance in Energy Provided (kWh) between two groups:

PAID vs ROAMING (Session Status) or weekend vs weekday.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\DocumentationTok{\#\#\# Variance/Spread Comparisons (F{-}Test) Significance}
\DocumentationTok{\#\#\# Level: α = 0.05}

\NormalTok{alpha }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{0.05}

\CommentTok{\#{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# 1. F{-}Test: PAID vs ROAMING (Session Status)}
\CommentTok{\#{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}

\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"===== F{-}TEST FOR VARIANCE: PAID vs ROAMING =====}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

        \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{p \textless{} 0.001"}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{fontface =} \StringTok{"bold"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{color =} \StringTok{"darkred"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{labs}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"Correlation: Charge Duration vs Energy Provided"}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{subtitle =} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"95\% CI for r: ["}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(cor\_test}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{conf.int[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{],}
            \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{", "}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(cor\_test}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{conf.int[}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{], }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{"]"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{sep =} \StringTok{""}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{x =} \StringTok{"Charge Duration (min)"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{y =} \StringTok{"Energy Provided (kWh)"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{theme\_minimal}\NormalTok{()}
\end{Highlighting}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Model: Energy Provided = β0 + β1(Charge Duration) + β2(is\_weekend) + β3(Location) + ε}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## Model: Energy Provided = β0 + β1(Charge Duration) + β2(is_weekend) + β3(Location) + ε
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Prepare data: select top 4 locations for cleaner model}
\NormalTok{top\_4\_locations }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ev\_data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{count}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{sort =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{head}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{pull}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{ev\_regression }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ev\_data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}} \SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ top\_4\_locations) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{Location =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Location Name}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{is\_weekend =} \FunctionTok{as.factor}\NormalTok{(is\_weekend))}

\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Locations included in model:}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\NormalTok{    is\_weekend }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ Location, }\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ ev\_regression)}

\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# 5. Coefficient Visualization}
\CommentTok{\#{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}

\CommentTok{\# Extract coefficients for visualization}
\NormalTok{coef\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{Term =} \FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{coef}\NormalTok{(multiple\_model)), }\AttributeTok{Estimate =} \FunctionTok{coef}\NormalTok{(multiple\_model),}
    \AttributeTok{CI\_lower =} \FunctionTok{confint}\NormalTok{(multiple\_model)[, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{], }\AttributeTok{CI\_upper =} \FunctionTok{confint}\NormalTok{(multiple\_model)[,}
        \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{]) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(Term }\SpecialCharTok{!=} \StringTok{"(Intercept)"}\NormalTok{)  }\CommentTok{\# Remove intercept for cleaner plot}

\CommentTok{\# Coefficient plot}
\FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(coef\_df, }\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =} \FunctionTok{reorder}\NormalTok{(Term, Estimate), }\AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ Estimate)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{geom\_point}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{color =} \StringTok{"steelblue"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{geom\_errorbar}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{ymin =}\NormalTok{ CI\_lower,}
    \AttributeTok{ymax =}\NormalTok{ CI\_upper), }\AttributeTok{width =} \FloatTok{0.2}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{color =} \StringTok{"steelblue"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{geom\_hline}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{yintercept =} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{linetype =} \StringTok{"dashed"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{color =} \StringTok{"red"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{coord\_flip}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{labs}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"Multiple Regression Coefficients"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{subtitle =} \StringTok{"With 95\% Confidence Intervals"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{x =} \StringTok{"Predictor"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{y =} \StringTok{"Coefficient Estimate"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{theme\_minimal}\NormalTok{()}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# 6. Summary Tables}
\CommentTok{\#{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}

\CommentTok{\# Correlation summary}
\NormalTok{correlation\_summary }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{Method =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Pearson"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Spearman"}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{Coefficient =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(cor\_test}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{estimate, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{), }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(cor\_test\_spearman}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{estimate,}
        \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{)), }\AttributeTok{p\_value =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{format}\NormalTok{(cor\_test}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p.value, }\AttributeTok{scientific =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
        \FunctionTok{format}\NormalTok{(cor\_test\_spearman}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p.value, }\AttributeTok{scientific =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)),}
    \AttributeTok{CI\_95 =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"("}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(cor\_test}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{conf.int[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{], }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{", "}\NormalTok{,}
        \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(cor\_test}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{conf.int[}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{], }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{")"}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{"N/A"}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{Decision =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(cor\_test}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p.value }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}}
\NormalTok{        alpha, }\StringTok{"Significant"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Not Significant"}\NormalTok{), }\FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(cor\_test\_spearman}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p.value }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}}
\NormalTok{        alpha, }\StringTok{"Significant"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Not Significant"}\NormalTok{)))}

\FunctionTok{kable}\NormalTok{(correlation\_summary, }\AttributeTok{caption =} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Correlation Test Summary (α ="}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{    alpha, }\StringTok{")"}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{col.names =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Method"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Coefficient"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"p{-}value"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"95\% CI"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Decision"}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{align =} \StringTok{"lrrrr"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{kable\_styling}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{bootstrap\_options =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"striped"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"hover"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"condensed"}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{full\_width =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{position =} \StringTok{"center"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

    \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{), }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(multiple\_summary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{r.squared, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{)), }\AttributeTok{Adj\_R\_squared =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(simple\_summary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{adj.r.squared,}
    \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{), }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(multiple\_summary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{adj.r.squared, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{)), }\AttributeTok{RSE =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(simple\_summary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{sigma,}
    \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{), }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(multiple\_summary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{sigma, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{)), }\AttributeTok{AIC =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{AIC}\NormalTok{(simple\_model\_subset),}
    \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{), }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{AIC}\NormalTok{(multiple\_model), }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{)), }\AttributeTok{BIC =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{BIC}\NormalTok{(simple\_model\_subset),}
    \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{), }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{BIC}\NormalTok{(multiple\_model), }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{)))}

        \StringTok{"BIC"}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{align =} \StringTok{"lrrrrr"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

        \AttributeTok{full\_width =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{position =} \StringTok{"center"}\NormalTok{)}
